**Assumptions and scope:** The conventional branch genuinely satisfies the same needs and control boundaries; this is not a general claim that agents are never useful.

**Novelty reason:** The stated global failure is already rejected by the listed inherited criterion or guarded inherited relation bundle. A new configuration, relation or failure witness is not by itself a new property identity.

**Evidence class:** ANALYTICAL_CONSTRUCTED_COUNTEREXAMPLE_NOT_EMPIRICAL

**Empirical validation:** false

**Local satisfaction warning:** Only the explicitly described local/projected checks are satisfied. No claim is made that all stronger inherited/global criteria hold; those identified as rejecting the failure are not silently counted as locally passed.

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ANALYTICAL_COUNTEREXAMPLES.json#/counterexamples/22`.

### AMA-X024 — Nominally identical repair changes the beneficiary and discharges the wrong promise

**Id:** AMA-X024

**Name:** Nominally identical repair changes the beneficiary and discharges the wrong promise

**Locally satisfied properties:** property: EAA:EAA-017; precise_local_pass: The replacement plan is technically feasible under its own named entity binding.; property: EMAS:EMAS-011; precise_local_pass: The replacement interaction has a legal completion path.; property: EAOSE:EAOSE-C058; precise_local_pass: The repaired code corresponds to that replacement plan.

**Composition level failure:** A repair keeps the task label “deliver report” but changes its beneficiary from A to B; a real delivery to B is credited against the unchanged promise to A.

**Inherited criteria rejecting failure:** EAA:EAA-010; EAA:EAA-067; EAA:EAA-069; EMAS:EMAS-010; EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-071; EAOSE:EAOSE-C002; EAOSE:EAOSE-C086; EAOSE:EAOSE-C098

**Assumptions and scope:** Technical plan validity is not authorised task equivalence. The case instantiates the already inherited same-decision and acceptance constraints.

**Novelty reason:** The stated global failure is already rejected by the listed inherited criterion or guarded inherited relation bundle. A new configuration, relation or failure witness is not by itself a new property identity.

**Evidence class:** ANALYTICAL_CONSTRUCTED_COUNTEREXAMPLE_NOT_EMPIRICAL

**Empirical validation:** false

**Local satisfaction warning:** Only the explicitly described local/projected checks are satisfied. No claim is made that all stronger inherited/global criteria hold; those identified as rejecting the failure are not silently counted as locally passed.

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ANALYTICAL_COUNTEREXAMPLES.json#/counterexamples/23`.


## All fifteen historical configurations

These are the September public source records, not the corrected r18 scientific findings. Source: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations` in the [immutable historical packet](https://github.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/blob/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/ama/packet/EVOLVED_AMA_FROZEN_PACKET.zip).

### AMA-CFG001 — Conventional non-agent implementation

**Id:** AMA-CFG001

**Name:** Conventional non-agent implementation

**Trigger:** An ordinary function, service, workflow or controller meets the actual requirements and control distribution at lower or otherwise preferable justified cost.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAOSE:EAOSE-C001; EAOSE:EAOSE-C002; EAOSE:EAOSE-C007; EAOSE:EAOSE-C012; EAOSE:EAOSE-C068; EAOSE:EAOSE-C098; EAOSE:EAOSE-C116; EAOSE:EAOSE-C117; EAA:EAA-020

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAOSE:EAOSE-C009; EAOSE:EAOSE-C039; EAOSE:EAOSE-C083; EAOSE:EAOSE-C106; EAOSE:EAOSE-C110

**Omitted mechanisms:** Autonomous goal selection; A multi-agent runtime; BDI state; Public-commitment middleware; Negotiation, swarms and generative agents

**Assumptions:** The simpler implementation genuinely meets the same relevant outcome, trust and authority needs; comparison does not give it unavailable central powers.

**Authority model:** Existing conventional permission, ownership and acceptance boundaries; no agency label adds or removes powers.

**Communication model:** Direct typed calls or ordinary service messages sufficient for the real dependency.

**Evidence requirements:** Requirement/acceptance evidence, credible comparator and complete relevant lifecycle costs; effect evidence appropriate to the action.

**Failure conditions:** Hidden independent principals, unhandled dynamic decisions or inter-party commitments exceed the conventional design.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Admit a richer branch only when a demonstrated capability/dependency/authority need warrants it and its affected evidence is revalidated.

**Omission and retirement path:** Keep this branch when adequate; remove residual agent-labelled machinery whose function has disappeared.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/0`.

### AMA-CFG002 — Agent-oriented analysis with conventional execution

**Id:** AMA-CFG002

**Name:** Agent-oriented analysis with conventional execution

**Trigger:** Actor/goal/dependency analysis clarifies the requirements, while autonomous runtime agents do not earn their implementation cost.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAOSE:EAOSE-C001; EAOSE:EAOSE-C002; EAOSE:EAOSE-C004; EAOSE:EAOSE-C012; EAOSE:EAOSE-C013; EAOSE:EAOSE-C058; EAOSE:EAOSE-C067; EAOSE:EAOSE-C068; EAA:EAA-020

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAOSE:EAOSE-C010; EAOSE:EAOSE-C061; EAOSE:EAOSE-C080; EAOSE:EAOSE-C106

**Omitted mechanisms:** One runtime process per modelled actor; Mandatory autonomous agent instances; Universal public commitments or BDI

**Assumptions:** The analysis-to-implementation mapping is explicit and does not pretend every stakeholder actor becomes software.

**Authority model:** Engineering actors validate requirements; operational permissions remain in the conventional implementation.

**Communication model:** Existing requirement decisions and conventional interfaces, not an invented agent conversation.

**Evidence requirements:** Evidence that relevant actor/goal concepts remain traceable and useful at the actual conventional decision/effect boundaries.

**Failure conditions:** The conceptual model has no consumer, or its obligations disappear in translation to code.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Revisit the abstraction/runtime choice when independent participants or adaptation alter the original assumptions.

**Omission and retirement path:** Retire unconsumed views; retain only the protected requirement and implementation correspondence.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/1`.

### AMA-CFG003 — Single bounded reactive agent

**Id:** AMA-CFG003

**Name:** Single bounded reactive agent

**Trigger:** Current observations are adequate for a timely bounded action without extensive hidden-state or long-horizon planning.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-001; EAA:EAA-004; EAA:EAA-005; EAA:EAA-010; EAA:EAA-016; EAA:EAA-021; EAA:EAA-023; EAA:EAA-026; EAOSE:EAOSE-C007; EAOSE:EAOSE-C084

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-006; EAA:EAA-009; EAA:EAA-022; EAA:EAA-025; EAA:EAA-040; EAA:EAA-045; EAOSE:EAOSE-C098

**Omitted mechanisms:** MAS organisation; General negotiation; Persistent BDI intentions; Complete central world model; Learning and reflection unless separately triggered

**Assumptions:** Observation/action mapping and response window are adequate; a minimal controller may satisfy the declared agency definition.

**Authority model:** Fixed bounded delegation with refusal for out-of-scope actions.

**Communication model:** Sensor/tool interface; direct human handoff only where consequential.

**Evidence requirements:** Observed effects, relevant dispatch conditions and deadline/resource performance in the intended environment.

**Failure conditions:** Perceptual aliasing, non-local goals, material delayed effects or conflicting activities exceed the simple policy.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Add state, planning, arbitration or an external participant only to address the demonstrated missing function.

**Omission and retirement path:** Return to a conventional controller when the agency distinction has no live engineering purpose.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/2`.

### AMA-CFG004 — Single deliberative or BDI plan-library agent

**Id:** AMA-CFG004

**Name:** Single deliberative or BDI plan-library agent

**Trigger:** Persistent structured goals, reusable plans or non-local contingencies justify explicit deliberation and intention management.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-003; EAA:EAA-010; EAA:EAA-013; EAA:EAA-017; EAA:EAA-021; EAA:EAA-023; EAA:EAA-024; EAA:EAA-026; EAOSE:EAOSE-C046; EAOSE:EAOSE-C047; EAOSE:EAOSE-C052

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-011; EAA:EAA-012; EAA:EAA-014; EAA:EAA-015; EAA:EAA-018; EAA:EAA-022; EAA:EAA-067; EAA:EAA-069; EAOSE:EAOSE-C054; EAOSE:EAOSE-C056

**Omitted mechanisms:** MAS task allocation without other actors; Public commitments for purely private plans; Mandatory learning or continuous reflection

**Assumptions:** Implemented interpreter semantics and plan-library coverage are appropriate; formal mental-state closure is not assumed for an arbitrary finite runtime.

**Authority model:** Goals have legitimate adoption and abandonment boundaries; plan alternatives remain within scope.

**Communication model:** Events and direct action/result interfaces; optional human clarification at genuine uncertainty.

**Evidence requirements:** Plan applicability/failure and goal-lifecycle tests, effect evidence and resource-aware comparison with a simpler workflow.

**Failure conditions:** Uncovered events, unjustified intention persistence, model mismatch or planning delay defeat the task.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Reconsider only on warranted triggers; join a collective branch only when real interdependence appears.

**Omission and retirement path:** Remove planning/BDI machinery when a stable simpler plan or state machine is adequate.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** selection: GUARDED_ARCHITECTURAL_VARIANT; options: EAA:EAA-017; EAA:EAA-018; rule: Explicit BDI machinery is required only for the BDI branch; model-based deliberation alone does not mandate a BDI interpreter.

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/3`.

### AMA-CFG005 — Reactive–deliberative or symbolic–learned hybrid

**Id:** AMA-CFG005

**Name:** Reactive–deliberative or symbolic–learned hybrid

**Trigger:** One task needs both timely reaction and slower planning or bounded learned capability.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-019; EAA:EAA-021; EAA:EAA-022; EAA:EAA-023; EAA:EAA-026; EAA:EAA-027; EAOSE:EAOSE-C053; EAOSE:EAOSE-C056; EAOSE:EAOSE-C058; EAOSE:EAOSE-C084; EAOSE:EAOSE-C128

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-007; EAA:EAA-017; EAA:EAA-032; EAA:EAA-033; EAA:EAA-050; EAA:EAA-067; EAOSE:EAOSE-C103; EAOSE:EAOSE-C129; EAOSE:EAOSE-C130

**Omitted mechanisms:** Unneeded architectural tiers; An assumption that combining layers combines only strengths; Automatic permission for learned outputs

**Assumptions:** Cross-layer timing, state/goal meanings, event arbitration and interruption semantics match the implementation.

**Authority model:** Fast and slow components share explicit bounded action powers; generation or learning cannot broaden them.

**Communication model:** Explicit layer interfaces carrying task identity, current conditions, completion/failure and overrides.

**Evidence requirements:** Cross-layer interference, dispatch/effect, responsiveness and changed-component regression evidence.

**Failure conditions:** Stale plans, conflicting priorities, blocked reactive duties or unvalidated learned outputs violate the assumed interface.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Revalidate affected interfaces on component change; use a justified fallback when planning/learning is unavailable.

**Omission and retirement path:** Remove a tier when a smaller controller meets the same conditions; learning remains optional.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/4`.

### AMA-CFG006 — Direct cooperative multi-agent team

**Id:** AMA-CFG006

**Name:** Direct cooperative multi-agent team

**Trigger:** A small set of genuinely distinct capable participants has aligned goals and a manageable shared dependency.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-004; EAA:EAA-021; EAA:EAA-023; EMAS:EMAS-001; EMAS:EMAS-002; EMAS:EMAS-003; EMAS:EMAS-004; EMAS:EMAS-016; EMAS:EMAS-020; EMAS:EMAS-027; EMAS:EMAS-061; EMAS:EMAS-069; EMAS:EMAS-073; EAOSE:EAOSE-C022; EAOSE:EAOSE-C085

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-015; EMAS:EMAS-018; EMAS:EMAS-019; EMAS:EMAS-023; EMAS:EMAS-024; EMAS:EMAS-028; EMAS:EMAS-059; EMAS:EMAS-062; EAOSE:EAOSE-C106

**Omitted mechanisms:** Universal auctions; Full mutual belief; Heavy public-commitment institutions; Learning or generative dialogue without a role

**Assumptions:** The cooperation, resource and information assumptions are real rather than inferred from role labels.

**Authority model:** Explicit participant rights, accepted responsibility and action permissions; an allocator cannot confer unavailable capabilities.

**Communication model:** Minimal direct coordination of the actual dependency, with mandatory failure/progress notification where relied upon.

**Evidence requirements:** Local effect and collective outcome evidence, constraint feasibility, participant and coordination failure tests and credible single-agent/conventional comparisons.

**Failure conditions:** Strategic behaviour, capability loss, shared-resource conflict or coordination-layer failure exceeds the selected regime.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Use EMAS-072-compatible handoff/revalidation when cooperation, membership or information assumptions change.

**Omission and retirement path:** Eliminate dependencies or consolidate agents when a smaller adequate arrangement preserves the protected contributions.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/5`.

### AMA-CFG007 — Public-commitment interaction among opaque principals

**Id:** AMA-CFG007

**Name:** Public-commitment interaction among opaque principals

**Trigger:** Independent participants need externally assessable conditional obligations without access to one another’s private mental state.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-036; EMAS:EMAS-004; EMAS:EMAS-007; EMAS:EMAS-008; EMAS:EMAS-010; EMAS:EMAS-012; EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-026; EMAS:EMAS-071; EAOSE:EAOSE-C033; EAOSE:EAOSE-C035; EAOSE:EAOSE-C041; EAOSE:EAOSE-C105

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-011; EMAS:EMAS-021; EMAS:EMAS-027; EMAS:EMAS-059; EMAS:EMAS-063; EAOSE:EAOSE-C034; EAOSE:EAOSE-C038; EAOSE:EAOSE-C039; EAOSE:EAOSE-C040; EAOSE:EAOSE-C109

**Omitted mechanisms:** A universal sincerity test over private beliefs; A mandatory private BDI architecture; Inference of execution from a legal conversation

**Assumptions:** Public parties, activation/discharge semantics, role binding and material observations are adequate for the selected obligation.

**Authority model:** Principals retain their own powers; creating an obligation does not automatically grant the debtor new permissions.

**Communication model:** Public commitment-changing information with sufficient correlation, alignment and version compatibility.

**Evidence requirements:** Evidence for public effects and actual fulfilment, unresolved delivery/effect status, and the assumptions behind any protocol liveness claim.

**Failure conditions:** Private-state dependence, unauthorised delegation, unobservable discharge or incompatible commitment views.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Use valid party-aware release, transfer or renegotiation; preserve live instances across version/role changes.

**Omission and retirement path:** Use a direct contract or ordinary interface when public commitment machinery is unnecessary; do not orphan live promises on retirement.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/6`.

### AMA-CFG008 — Role- and organisation-based collective

**Id:** AMA-CFG008

**Name:** Role- and organisation-based collective

**Trigger:** Shared responsibilities, multiple role bindings or organisation-level constraints cannot be adequately captured by isolated local tasks.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-004; EAA:EAA-012; EMAS:EMAS-036; EMAS:EMAS-037; EMAS:EMAS-039; EAOSE:EAOSE-C020; EAOSE:EAOSE-C021; EAOSE:EAOSE-C022; EAOSE:EAOSE-C025; EAOSE:EAOSE-C031; EAOSE:EAOSE-C058; EAOSE:EAOSE-C085

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-038; EMAS:EMAS-040; EAOSE:EAOSE-C023; EAOSE:EAOSE-C024; EAOSE:EAOSE-C026; EAOSE:EAOSE-C029; EAOSE:EAOSE-C105

**Omitted mechanisms:** One-role/one-process assumption; Holonic decomposition where flat structure is adequate; Role charts as evidence of enacted organisation

**Assumptions:** Responsibilities, permissions, capabilities, role cardinalities and organisation constraints have compatible operational meanings.

**Authority model:** Role allocation is separate from delegation standing, current permission and actual capability.

**Communication model:** Role-relevant interaction contracts and state needed for collective constraints; not a mandatory communication volume.

**Evidence requirements:** Role-to-implementation evidence, organisation-level constraint tests, capability/permission checks and final collective consequences.

**Failure conditions:** Locally valid roles conflict globally, a role lacks a performer, or diagrams have no operative consumer.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Bind/rebind concrete principals explicitly and apply a separate live-obligation transition check.

**Omission and retirement path:** Flatten or remove organisational layers when their constraints and consumers can be preserved more simply.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/7`.

### AMA-CFG009 — Adaptive or reconfigurable multi-agent organisation

**Id:** AMA-CFG009

**Name:** Adaptive or reconfigurable multi-agent organisation

**Trigger:** Membership, capabilities, partner policies or organisational needs change in ways a static compatible arrangement cannot handle adequately.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-029; EAA:EAA-032; EAA:EAA-055; EAA:EAA-067; EAA:EAA-069; EMAS:EMAS-040; EMAS:EMAS-063; EMAS:EMAS-072; EMAS:EMAS-074; EAOSE:EAOSE-C024; EAOSE:EAOSE-C103; EAOSE:EAOSE-C104; EAOSE:EAOSE-C105

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-021; EMAS:EMAS-026; EMAS:EMAS-027; EMAS:EMAS-050; EMAS:EMAS-059; EAOSE:EAOSE-C040; EAOSE:EAOSE-C072; EAOSE:EAOSE-C102; EAOSE:EAOSE-C109

**Omitted mechanisms:** Unrestricted self-revision; Automatic validity of successor guarantees; Erasure of live obligations on departure

**Assumptions:** Material dependency changes can be detected or explicitly left uncertain; a legitimate transition actor and valid responsibility disposition exist.

**Authority model:** Adaptation is bounded by changed object, actor, trigger and preserved constraints; destination structure does not itself authorise transition.

**Communication model:** Explicit regime-change and in-flight obligation information to affected capable decision makers.

**Evidence requirements:** Changed-premise analysis, targeted regression, partner/resource/commitment compatibility and traceable transition effects.

**Failure conditions:** Unnoticed assumption drift, unavailable successor capability, self-approved objective change or stale assurance.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Preserve or validly change each affected dependency/obligation; suspend unsupported claims when a compatible transition cannot be established.

**Omission and retirement path:** Retire adaptation/reorganisation mechanisms when the environment is sufficiently stable and simpler continuity checks suffice.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/8`.

### AMA-CFG010 — Mixed human–agent collaborative system

**Id:** AMA-CFG010

**Name:** Mixed human–agent collaborative system

**Trigger:** People have relevant knowledge, preference, legitimacy or decision rights that cannot be adequately delegated away.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-037; EAA:EAA-038; EAA:EAA-039; EAA:EAA-040; EAA:EAA-043; EMAS:EMAS-068; EAOSE:EAOSE-C006; EAOSE:EAOSE-C091; EAOSE:EAOSE-C106; EAOSE:EAOSE-C110

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-009; EAA:EAA-044; EMAS:EMAS-027; EMAS:EMAS-059; EAOSE:EAOSE-C001; EAOSE:EAOSE-C039; EAOSE:EAOSE-C111

**Omitted mechanisms:** Human approval as universal reassurance; Instant or infallible takeover assumption; Silence as unrestricted consent

**Assumptions:** The intended person can receive, interpret and act on the relevant information within a justified response window, or non-response is handled explicitly.

**Authority model:** Separate advice, proposal, approval, refusal, revocation and task transfer; identify what the human actually controls.

**Communication model:** Minimal usable evidence, options and consequences at the right decision boundary; no ceremonial approval messages.

**Evidence requirements:** Usability/fidelity evidence, authority reaching dispatch, observed cancellation effect or inability, and explicit supervision/attention costs.

**Failure conditions:** Late unusable explanations, approval after irreversible dispatch, unavailable humans or unaccounted unfulfilled obligations.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Transfer only when genuine readiness and live-work disposition support it; retain bounded fallback/non-action semantics.

**Omission and retirement path:** Remove unhelpful approvals within adequate delegated scope while preserving legitimately retained human rights.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/9`.

### AMA-CFG011 — Higher-assurance bounded agent subset

**Id:** AMA-CFG011

**Name:** Higher-assurance bounded agent subset

**Trigger:** Consequences and tractable assumptions justify formal checking, strong interfaces and targeted adversarial testing for a selected subset of behaviour.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-022; EAA:EAA-023; EAA:EAA-045; EAA:EAA-049; EAA:EAA-050; EAA:EAA-051; EMAS:EMAS-058; EAOSE:EAOSE-C058; EAOSE:EAOSE-C081; EAOSE:EAOSE-C082; EAOSE:EAOSE-C083; EAOSE:EAOSE-C084; EAOSE:EAOSE-C086; EAOSE:EAOSE-C089

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-011; EMAS:EMAS-043; EMAS:EMAS-044; EMAS:EMAS-075; EAOSE:EAOSE-C023; EAOSE:EAOSE-C059; EAOSE:EAOSE-C088; EAOSE:EAOSE-C099; EAOSE:EAOSE-C103; EAOSE:EAOSE-C129

**Omitted mechanisms:** Exhaustive unrestricted behaviour assurance; Automatic deployment certificates from code generation or model checking; Assurance of unobserved assumptions by assertion

**Assumptions:** The property, model, information/fairness/fault conditions, implementation relation and operating abstraction are justified for the declared claim.

**Authority model:** Permission and protected outcomes are explicit constraints, not created by proof or model syntax.

**Communication model:** Interfaces have semantics adequate to the checked assumption/guarantee; public/private access limits remain explicit.

**Evidence requirements:** Exact model and bounds, correspondence/abstraction evidence, relevant perturbation tests and runtime responses to material observable assumption violations.

**Failure conditions:** The environment leaves the model, an implementation is nonconforming, or a narrow theorem is promoted to unrestricted safety/optimality.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Revalidate affected premises and select a justified degraded branch or suspend the unsupported guarantee.

**Omission and retirement path:** Reduce checking scope when consequences/dependencies no longer justify the burden; do not retire the protected requirement.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/10`.

### AMA-CFG012 — Bounded generative or LLM-agent configuration

**Id:** AMA-CFG012

**Name:** Bounded generative or LLM-agent configuration

**Trigger:** Language-mediated variability or useful plan/tool generation earns its cost over fixed workflows, libraries and simpler agents in the declared task.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-021; EAA:EAA-022; EAA:EAA-023; EAA:EAA-045; EAA:EAA-046; EAA:EAA-052; EAA:EAA-053; EAA:EAA-055; EMAS:EMAS-048; EMAS:EMAS-065; EMAS:EMAS-066; EMAS:EMAS-067; EAOSE:EAOSE-C123; EAOSE:EAOSE-C124; EAOSE:EAOSE-C125; EAOSE:EAOSE-C126; EAOSE:EAOSE-C129

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-030; EAA:EAA-031; EAA:EAA-054; EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-027; EMAS:EMAS-073; EMAS:EMAS-074; EAOSE:EAOSE-C127; EAOSE:EAOSE-C128; EAOSE:EAOSE-C130

**Omitted mechanisms:** Role prose as enforced contract; Unbounded tool authority; Mandatory multi-agent debate or swarms; Automatic reflection after every event; Generated plans as proofs

**Assumptions:** Models, prompts, tools, policies and environment versions are identified; evaluation has adequate external oracles and credible resource comparisons.

**Authority model:** Generated intentions and retrieved/peer text are proposals or evidence, never self-created authority; constrained actions remain externally governed.

**Communication model:** Mechanism-specific messages with provenance, identity and actual information value; one agent is an admissible branch.

**Evidence requirements:** Repeated task/seed/partner/version outcomes including failures, policy/effect checks, prompt-injection tests within the frozen claim scope, and response-time evidence.

**Failure conditions:** Hallucinated state, copied false evidence, blocked runtime, unearned agent count, stale versions or invalid generated protocol/role semantics.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Revalidate material model/tool/protocol changes and preserve task, partner and obligation context during repair.

**Omission and retirement path:** Replace generation or extra agents with an adequate fixed mechanism when its comparative function disappears.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/11`.

### AMA-CFG013 — Strategic allocation among independent participants

**Id:** AMA-CFG013

**Name:** Strategic allocation among independent participants

**Trigger:** Distinct incentives, ownership or private information materially affect task allocation or cooperation.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-004; EAA:EAA-034; EMAS:EMAS-003; EMAS:EMAS-004; EMAS:EMAS-030; EMAS:EMAS-032; EMAS:EMAS-033; EMAS:EMAS-034; EAOSE:EAOSE-C005; EAOSE:EAOSE-C006; EAOSE:EAOSE-C009; EAOSE:EAOSE-C030

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-015; EMAS:EMAS-017; EMAS:EMAS-021; EMAS:EMAS-031; EMAS:EMAS-035; EMAS:EMAS-047; EMAS:EMAS-060; EMAS:EMAS-072; EAOSE:EAOSE-C088

**Omitted mechanisms:** Universal cooperation assumption; Equilibrium as welfare, fairness or legitimacy certificate; Automatic global optimality from local trading

**Assumptions:** The game, information structure, participation constraints and chosen solution concept match the analysed regime.

**Authority model:** Principals’ valid powers and participation rights are separate from strategic stability and payment/allocation rules.

**Communication model:** Only the bids, offers, commitments and observations needed by the selected mechanism, with disclosed information/incentive assumptions.

**Evidence requirements:** Model-scoped incentive/feasibility claims, deviation and opponent-shift tests, resource costs and separately assessed accepted external outcomes.

**Failure conditions:** False reporting outside the mechanism assumptions, collusion, incompatible budget/efficiency demands or changing opponents.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Use explicit regime guards when incentive or information assumptions change; do not retain cooperative guarantees by inertia.

**Omission and retirement path:** Use direct allocation instead when ownership and suitable execution are already determined and negotiation adds no value.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** []

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/12`.

### AMA-CFG014 — Distributed constraint or optimisation configuration

**Id:** AMA-CFG014

**Name:** Distributed constraint or optimisation configuration

**Trigger:** Distributed variable/resource ownership and explicit encoded joint constraints justify a selected DCSP/DCOP algorithm.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-007; EAA:EAA-026; EAA:EAA-051; EMAS:EMAS-002; EMAS:EMAS-004; EMAS:EMAS-020; EAOSE:EAOSE-C023; EAOSE:EAOSE-C082; EAOSE:EAOSE-C083; EAOSE:EAOSE-C086

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-043; EMAS:EMAS-044; EMAS:EMAS-045; EMAS:EMAS-058; EMAS:EMAS-062; EMAS:EMAS-075; EAOSE:EAOSE-C009; EAOSE:EAOSE-C056; EAOSE:EAOSE-C108

**Omitted mechanisms:** A claim that local termination is global optimum; Fault tolerance stronger than the selected model; Unnecessary algorithm families not selected for the problem

**Assumptions:** Select a specific satisfaction or optimisation branch; only its relevant EMAS-043/044 obligations activate. Transport/search/width/memory/quality assumptions and objective adequacy remain explicit.

**Authority model:** Variable ownership and technical computation do not create permission for all suggested external actions.

**Communication model:** Algorithm-specific assignments, constraints, nogoods or optimisation messages with timing, disclosure and replica assumptions stated.

**Evidence requirements:** The returned assignment/quality claim under its exact model, resource and privacy costs, implementation validity and separate accepted external consequence.

**Failure conditions:** An encoded optimum optimises the wrong objective, hidden coupling invalidates the constraints, or communication/fault assumptions fail.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Rebuild or revalidate affected constraints and guarantees when population, objective, protocol or fault regime changes.

**Omission and retirement path:** Use central/direct allocation or remove dependencies when the distributed solver’s added cost is not justified.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** selection: ONE_APPLICABLE_FORMULATION; options: EMAS:EMAS-043; EMAS:EMAS-044; rule: Select satisfaction or optimisation according to the actual problem; exact-optimality or replica guarantees require their separate assumptions.

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/13`.

### AMA-CFG015 — Argumentation or collective-choice decision support

**Id:** AMA-CFG015

**Name:** Argumentation or collective-choice decision support

**Trigger:** Participants must exchange reasons or aggregate preferences under a chosen public decision semantics rather than merely coordinate an already-agreed task.

**Required mechanism properties:** EAA:EAA-005; EAA:EAA-010; EAA:EAA-045; EMAS:EMAS-007; EMAS:EMAS-012; EMAS:EMAS-048; EAOSE:EAOSE-C002; EAOSE:EAOSE-C006; EAOSE:EAOSE-C087; EAOSE:EAOSE-C106

**Conditional mechanism properties:** EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-030; EMAS:EMAS-046; EMAS:EMAS-047; EMAS:EMAS-068; EAOSE:EAOSE-C033; EAOSE:EAOSE-C035; EAOSE:EAOSE-C091; EAOSE:EAOSE-C110

**Omitted mechanisms:** Consensus as truth; Permissible dialogue moves as guaranteed termination; A universal voting rule satisfying incompatible ideals

**Assumptions:** Use a specified argumentation/dialogue semantics, or activate the appropriate voting-domain/manipulation criteria when preference aggregation is selected; neither is universal.

**Authority model:** Acceptance of an argument or vote outcome has only the legitimate decision effect assigned to it; it does not create external rights.

**Communication model:** Defined assertion/challenge/support/acceptance and update moves, or a disclosed voting interface, with evidence dependence preserved.

**Evidence requirements:** Acceptability or selection under the chosen semantics, independent factual evidence where factual claims are made, and the actual consequential consumer/outcome.

**Failure conditions:** An accepted argument is factually false, repeated sources manufacture consensus, dialogue never progresses, or legitimate affected parties are excluded.

**Transition and reconfiguration rules:** Revise the chosen semantics or participant scope only through the appropriate authorised decision; preserve live obligations and unresolved evidence.

**Omission and retirement path:** Use a simpler direct decision or factual check when dialogue or preference aggregation has no consequential function.

**Validation status:** ANALYTICAL_GUARDED_CONFIGURATION_NOT_WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICALLY_VALIDATED

**Mechanism selection groups:** selection: CHOOSE_ACTUAL_DECISION_SEMANTICS; options: EMAS:EMAS-046; EMAS:EMAS-047; rule: Argument acceptability and preference aggregation are different branches; each activates only its own applicable semantic/domain limits.

Exact original selector: `EVOLVED_AMA_ALTERNATIVE_CONFIGURATIONS.json#/configurations/14`.

### AMA-N001 — Consequential goal–authority–obligation–effect join

**Id:** AMA-N001

**Name:** Consequential goal–authority–obligation–effect join

**Hypothesised class:** THREE_WAY_EAA_EMAS_EAOSE

**Parent properties:** EAA:EAA-010; EAA:EAA-022; EAA:EAA-067; EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-071; EAOSE:EAOSE-C002; EAOSE:EAOSE-C007; EAOSE:EAOSE-C086; EAOSE:EAOSE-C098

**Candidate bearer:** The composed decision/configuration and its claim-specific cross-level relations; not a renamed individual agent.

**Candidate criterion:** Do not justify one consequential dispatch or acceptance by unrelated goal, scope, commitment, capability or effect records; the material bindings and claim-specific evidence must be compatible.

**Relational derivation:** Local current action basis meets public interaction/obligation semantics and engineering acceptance. The composition exposes the join obligations and the distinction between permission to attempt and evidence to accept.

**Inherited subsumers:** EAA:EAA-067; EMAS:EMAS-071; EAOSE:EAOSE-C086; EAOSE:EAOSE-C002; EAOSE:EAOSE-C007; EAOSE:EAOSE-C098

**Distinctness adjudication:** EAA-067 already excludes splicing individually valid facts into a nonexistent action basis; EMAS-071 already separates evidence, obligation, intervention and consequence. Canonical EAOSE-RB01 expressly applies C086/C002/C007/C058/C098/C067 to one subject/action/configuration/time/acceptance context. Renaming that existing bundle at AMA level creates no residual criterion.

**Trigger:** Consequential cross-level action or acceptance with multiple sources of state, scope or effect.

**Cheap or non trigger path:** A single adequate atomic permission/action/result contract may establish the relevant relation without an extra join procedure.

**Decision:** RELATION_ONLY_OR_INHERITED_SUBSUMPTION

**Minimality result:** NOT_ADMITTED: distinctness or assurance-support test fails; no surviving AMA-E arity exists.

### AMA-N002 — Cross-level intention and obligation continuity

**Id:** AMA-N002

**Name:** Cross-level intention and obligation continuity

**Hypothesised class:** THREE_WAY_EAA_EMAS_EAOSE

**Parent properties:** EAA:EAA-013; EAA:EAA-015; EMAS:EMAS-023; EMAS:EMAS-025; EMAS:EMAS-026; EAOSE:EAOSE-C051; EAOSE:EAOSE-C105

**Candidate bearer:** The composed decision/configuration and its claim-specific cross-level relations; not a renamed individual agent.

**Candidate criterion:** A private change of intention must not silently discharge, orphan or transfer a public obligation or persisting organisational responsibility.

**Relational derivation:** Distinguish the local intention lifecycle from the public commitment lifecycle and the valid organisational transition between responsible principals.

**Inherited subsumers:** EMAS:EMAS-023; EMAS:EMAS-026; EMAS:EMAS-027; EAOSE:EAOSE-C051; EAOSE:EAOSE-C105

**Distinctness adjudication:** C105 explicitly requires retaining, validly transferring, releasing, compensating or reporting inability for outstanding public obligations at reassignment; C051 already asks what happened to dependent obligations when intention changes. MAS persistence/alignment supplies the social mechanics. This is a new cross-level application, not a new property.

**Trigger:** Intention withdrawal, loss of local relevance or changing role holder affects relied-on work.

**Cheap or non trigger path:** No public transition machinery is needed for a purely private goal with no dependent responsibility.

**Decision:** RELATION_ONLY_OR_INHERITED_SUBSUMPTION

**Minimality result:** NOT_ADMITTED: distinctness or assurance-support test fails; no surviving AMA-E arity exists.

**Candidate bearer:** The composed decision/configuration and its claim-specific cross-level relations; not a renamed individual agent.

**Candidate criterion:** Evidence needed by a consequential acceptance or intervention must be available in usable form to an authorised actor capable of making that decision in time, or the claim remains open.

**Relational derivation:** Distinguish local observation adequacy and distributed provenance from the information, authority and practical ability of the actual consequential consumer.

**Inherited subsumers:** EMAS:EMAS-004; EMAS:EMAS-071; EAOSE:EAOSE-C106

**Distinctness adjudication:** C106 states the proposed criterion directly, including reachability, authority, capability, usability and legitimate omission. Calling it epistemic reachability changes terminology, not its property identity. Information privacy may make the condition infeasible; that is an exposed configuration failure, not an assurance theorem.

**Trigger:** An acceptance or intervention depends on evidence held elsewhere, at training time only, or behind a disclosure boundary.

**Cheap or non trigger path:** One existing sufficient permitted evidence interface is enough; omit an unneeded handoff.

**Decision:** RELATION_ONLY_OR_INHERITED_SUBSUMPTION

**Candidate criterion:** Generated roles, plans, protocols or lessons must have bounded operational interpretation, authority, provenance, responsiveness and claim-appropriate repeated evaluation before stronger effects are credited.

**Relational derivation:** Combine observation-responsive tool use, actual collective mechanisms and engineering realisation/authority/temporal constraints; keep generated proposals distinct from enacted obligations and effects.

**Cheap or non trigger path:** Use fixed plan libraries, ordinary services or one bounded agent when a generative collective adds no justified value.

**Candidate bearer:** The composed decision/configuration and its claim-specific cross-level relations; not a renamed individual agent.

**Candidate criterion:** Individually useful reasoning, coordination and assurance must still fit the actual whole-task/lifecycle cost and obligation envelope; excess machinery may be simplified or retired.

**Relational derivation:** Bring local reasoning cost, collective communication/integration burden and engineering lifecycle effort into the same declared comparison without assuming one universal additive scalar.

**Inherited subsumers:** EAA:EAA-048; EMAS:EMAS-065; EMAS:EMAS-073; EAOSE:EAOSE-C009; EAOSE:EAOSE-C057; EAOSE:EAOSE-C068; EAOSE:EAOSE-C117

The 4+4+4&gt;10 example exposes an omitted global budget, not a new criterion beyond those inherited.

**Trigger:** Added agents, coordination or assurance consume a shared resource or displace costs to other phases/actors.

**Cheap or non trigger path:** Keep incomparable burdens visible and choose a cheaper adequate branch; do not minimise mechanism count at the expense of required outcomes.

**Decision:** RELATION_ONLY_OR_INHERITED_SUBSUMPTION
